    "client_id": "photo-1", "filename": "photo.jpg", "size": 1024,
    "sha256": "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa",
    "remote_filename": "photo.jpg", "remote_relative_path": "photo.jpg",
    "remote_image_id": 501, "matched_by": "checksum_sha256"
  }],
  "deep_check_applied": false,
  "fingerprint": "opaque-server-fingerprint",
  "server_time": "2026-11-09 10:00:00",
  "capabilities": {"flight_reports_v1": true}
}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 returns \codeword{ok}, \codeword{action}, integer \codeword{gallery_id}, string
\codeword{gallery_title} and \codeword{gallery_folder}, integer \codeword{checked} and
\codeword{matched}, an \codeword{existing} list, boolean \codeword{deep_check_applied},
opaque \codeword{fingerprint}, SQL-style \codeword{server_time}, and~optional
\codeword{capabilities}. Each match echoes \codeword{client_id}/filename/size/sha256
and~supplies
\codeword{remote_filename}/\codeword{remote_relative_path}/\codeword{remote_image_id}/\codeword{matched_by}.
Paths are~metadata, not~public media URLs. \codeword{checksum_sha256} denotes an~indexed
match; \codeword{disk_sha256} can~have \codeword{remote_image_id=0}. No row means
no~observed match, not~proof that a~previous request never committed. Keep fingerprints
opaque; SQL-style timestamps carry no~explicit timezone offset.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Partial results, timeouts and~recovery}{Partial results, timeouts and recovery}}

\label{api352:recovery}

After a~confirmed success, retain the~receipt and~inspect warnings. After a~timeout,
disconnect, malformed response or~truncated JSON, mark the~upload outcome unknown and~retain
the~original file. Do not~resend automatically, delete local files, change the~target,
or~assume that 422 implies rollback. Probe inventory with the~original bytes and, only when
necessary, a~small explicit \codeword{deep_check} request. A~positive match observes
content, not~request-specific completion; a~disk match does not~prove database registration.
Reconcile the~gallery and~warnings before an~operator deliberately decides whether another
upload is~appropriate. This protocol exposes no~general photo-upload operation-status
receipt, cancellation endpoint, universal Idempotency-Key or~exactly-once guarantee.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Revoke the~current key}{Revoke the current key}}

\label{api352:revoke}

Operation: POST to~the~upload endpoint, \codeword{action=revoke}, as~JSON or~URL-encoded
form. No \codeword{token_id} selector is~accepted for~this action: it~revokes the~credential
authenticating this request, for~its gallery. The~key cannot create a~replacement or~revoke
arbitrary other keys. Stop and~reconcile pending work first. This is~an~explicit destructive
credential action; never run it~as~a~routine connection test. It does not~delete photos.
Subsequent use of~the~revoked key receives 401 on~the~enabled upload route; replay
is~not~a~second successful revocation receipt.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
# OPTIONAL: this invalidates the currently supplied key.
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 30 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_API_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --data-urlencode 'action=revoke' \
  --write-out '\nHTTP %{http_code}\n' "$API_URL"
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok": true, "action": "revoke", "gallery_id": 42,
 "token_id": 7, "message": "API key revoked."}
\end{lstlisting}

Success is~HTTP 200. A~failed revocation returns 422 with ok=false/error. Losing
the~response requires checking current key usability or~Admin key state; it~does
not~authorize a~new key automatically.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Specialized AI worker contract}{Specialized AI worker contract}}

\label{api352:ai-contract}

The~following four actions reuse the~upload endpoint and~gallery key, but~are~specialized
worker operations, not~generic photo browsing. The~installation must enable
\codeword{upload_api} and \codeword{ai_image_metadata} and~have the~AI queue/metadata
schema. The~worker must hold the~current \codeword{job_id} plus the~secret
\codeword{claim_token} as~well as~the~gallery key. Claims are~scoped to~that gallery;
\codeword{worker_id} is~diagnostic, not~authentication. Do not~log claim tokens. No
automatic browser, filesystem crawler or~perpetual polling loop is~part of~these examples.

Common AI responses: 403 if~AI metadata is~disabled; 409 if~its schema is~missing; 503
if~storage cannot be~verified; 400 for~an~unknown ai\_ action; 422 for~other caught worker
failures. The~shared upload authentication/feature refusals still precede these checks.
A~lost claim response is~not~permission to~complete another job: only a~received claim token
proves possession. Completion retry semantics described below do~not~make photo uploads
idempotent.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Claim one AI job: \codeword{ai_next_job}}{Claim one AI job: ai\_next\_job}}

\label{api352:ai-next-job}

POST, application/json, action=\codeword{ai_next_job}. This is~a~state-changing allocation:
missing jobs may~be~enqueued and~one job claimed for~direct images in~the~key gallery. It
is~not~a~read-only connection probe.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{worker_id} & Optional string; default unknown-worker; unsupported characters
replaced with underscore, at~most 190 characters. \\
\codeword{model_name} / \codeword{model_version} & Optional strings; defaults
local-image-metadata / 1; whitespace collapsed, at~most 120 characters each. Keep stable
to~identify the~intended model/version queue. \\
\codeword{lease_seconds} & Optional integer seconds, default 600. Nonpositive values use
default; otherwise clamped to~60..3600. Same normalization applies to~heartbeat. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"action":"ai_next_job","worker_id":"example-worker",
 "model_name":"local-image-metadata","model_version":"1",
 "lease_seconds":600}
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{
  "ok": true, "asset_action": "ai_asset",
  "job": {
    "job_id": 91, "claim_token": "REPLACE_WITH_RECEIVED_CLAIM_TOKEN",
    "lease_seconds": 600, "lease_expires_at": "2026-11-09 10:10:00",
    "model_name": "local-image-metadata", "model_version": "1",
    "gallery_id": 42, "gallery_title": "Example",
    "image": {
      "id": 501, "filename": "photo.jpg", "title": "Example",
      "relative_path": "photo.jpg", "mime_type": "image/jpeg",
      "file_size": 1024, "modified_at": "2026-11-09 09:00:00",
      "checksum_sha256": "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa",
      "width": 1600, "height": 900
    }
  }
}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 returns a~job object with the~complete fields illustrated above, or~job=null with
\codeword{poll_after_seconds}=60 and~a~message. Integer fields are \codeword{job_id},
\codeword{lease_seconds}, \codeword{gallery_id}, \codeword{image.id},
\codeword{image.file_size}, \codeword{image.width}, \codeword{image.height}; other shown
job/image fields are~strings. Respect the~suggested idle delay and~the~lease deadline.
The~placeholder is~deliberately not~a~valid claim token.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Renew an~AI claim: \codeword{ai_heartbeat}}{Renew an AI claim: ai\_heartbeat}}

\label{api352:ai-heartbeat}

POST, application/json, action=\codeword{ai_heartbeat}. Extends the~current live claim;
it~cannot revive an~expired or~foreign claim.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{job_id} / \codeword{claim_token} & Required positive integer and~exact secret
string from the~claim response. The~gallery key is~still required in~the~header. \\
\codeword{lease_seconds} & Optional; same 600 default and~60..3600 second bounds as~claim.
\\
\codeword{progress_percent} & Optional integer; default 0, clamped to~0..99. Completion
is~a~separate action. \\
\codeword{message} & Optional string; default Worker heartbeat.; stored text limited to~500
characters. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"action":"ai_heartbeat","job_id":91,
 "claim_token":"REPLACE_WITH_RECEIVED_CLAIM_TOKEN",
 "lease_seconds":600,"progress_percent":40,"message":"Analyzing image"}
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"job_id":91,"message":"Heartbeat accepted."}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 confirms renewal; 409 returns ok=false/error when the~claim is~invalid or~expired.
Common AI errors also apply.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Complete or~fail an~AI job: \codeword{ai_complete}}{Complete or fail an AI job: ai\_complete}}

\label{api352:ai-complete}

POST, application/json, action=\codeword{ai_complete}. Success stores model metadata
and~searchable text; it~is~not~an~arbitrary photo-title/tag/settings mutation API.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{job_id} / \codeword{claim_token} & Required claim identity, as~for~heartbeat. \\
\codeword{status} & Optional string; default succeeded. Send succeeded or~failed explicitly.
Current handler treats every other value as~failure; do~not~rely on~misspellings. \\
\codeword{metadata} & Success: JSON object, default empty. Worker-owned structured metadata,
no~fixed required tag vocabulary. Non-object/non-array values fail with 422. Do not~store
secrets or~diagnostics as~searchable content. \\
\codeword{searchable_text} & Success: optional string, default empty. Combined with scalar
metadata text to~depth 5, diagnostic keys excluded, whitespace normalized; final text
limited to~60000 characters. \\
\codeword{error} & Failure: optional string, limited to~2000 characters. A~server fallback
message is~used when omitted. Do not~include credentials. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"action":"ai_complete","job_id":91,
 "claim_token":"REPLACE_WITH_RECEIVED_CLAIM_TOKEN",
 "status":"succeeded","metadata":{"objects":["aircraft"]},
 "searchable_text":"Aircraft on the apron"}
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"job_id":91,"status":"succeeded","message":"AI metadata stored."}
\end{lstlisting}

Failure acknowledgement is~also HTTP 200 with status=failed and~message="AI job failure
recorded."; it~does not~mean analysis succeeded. An invalid/expired claim gives 409.
Repeating a~successful completion with the~same already-succeeded job/claim can~acknowledge
the~stored result without replacing it; this does not~verify that a~repeated payload
is~identical. A~failure may~be~requeued by~server policy (maximum 5 attempts, exponential
backoff capped at~3600 seconds). The~client must not~manufacture a~new claim or~blindly
resubmit after 409.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Fetch the~claimed AI input: \codeword{ai_asset}}{Fetch the claimed AI input: ai\_asset}}

\label{api352:ai-asset}

POST to~the~upload endpoint using application/x-www-form-urlencoded (multipart also
populates the~required form fields). Required fields are~action=\codeword{ai_asset},
positive integer \codeword{job_id} and~the~exact \codeword{claim_token}. Unlike the~other AI
actions, this handler reads \codeword{job_id} and \codeword{claim_token} from form data,
not~JSON. Success is~HTTP 200 with image bytes, detected Content-Type, Content-Length,
Content-Disposition attachment, X-Content-Type-Options nosniff and~Cache-Control no-store,
private. The~server can~return an~existing display representation rather than original RAW
bytes; do~not~assume its hash equals the~original checksum in~the~job. It does not~generate
a~missing derivative just to~serve this action.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
# CLAIM_TOKEN and JOB_ID come from a successful ai_next_job response.
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_API_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --data-urlencode 'action=ai_asset' \
  --data-urlencode "job_id=$JOB_ID" \
  --data-urlencode "claim_token=$CLAIM_TOKEN" \
  --dump-header ai-asset.headers --output ai-asset.response \
  --write-out 'HTTP %{http_code}\n' "$API_URL"
\end{lstlisting}

An invalid claim or~unavailable asset returns JSON 409 with ok=false/error; other common AI
failures remain JSON. Check HTTP status and~Content-Type before treating the~saved response
as~an~image.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Look up~a~PDF report receipt: \codeword{flight_report_status}}{Look up a PDF report receipt: flight\_report\_status}}

\label{api352:flight-report-status}

POST to~the~upload endpoint, application/json, action=\codeword{flight_report_status}. This
is~the~specialized report attachment protocol, not~unrestricted PDF management.
The~inventory response can~advertise capabilities.\codeword{flight_reports_v1}=true; older
responses may~omit capabilities. That advertisement is~not~a~readiness guarantee. On
the~audited revision it~is~unconditional after successful inventory and~does
not~independently verify PDF storage or~the~PDF presentation switch. Do not~infer other
permissions or~UI availability from it.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{report_id} & Required string, 1..80 ASCII characters [A-Za-z0-9\_-]. Stable caller
identity reused for~lookup and~upload. \\
\codeword{language} & Required string en/cs/de/sv, normalized to~lowercase. The~Czech wire
code is~cs, not~the~manual filename suffix CZ. \\
\codeword{sha256} & Required 64-hex digest of~the~expected PDF; trimmed and~lowercased.
Identity is \codeword{report_id} + language within the~key gallery. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"action":"flight_report_status","report_id":"flight-demo-001",
 "language":"cs",
 "sha256":"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa"}
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"action":"flight_report_status","found":false,
 "conflict":false,"report":null}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 has~found boolean, conflict=false, and~report=null when no~verified attachment
is~observed, otherwise report=\{id:string, sha256:string, size:integer bytes,
filename:string\}. A~matching receipt has~found=true. The~lookup is~based on~verified
indexed files; found=false is~not~proof that no~lost write ever happened. Conflicting
content or~duplicate identities return HTTP 409 with ok=false, action, conflict=true
and~error. Invalid input or~unverifiable status returns 422; a~missing/non-JSON status
payload returns 400. No overwrite flag is~offered.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Attach a~PDF report: \codeword{flight_report}}{Attach a PDF report: flight\_report}}

\label{api352:flight-report}

POST to~the~upload endpoint, multipart/form-data, action=\codeword{flight_report}. Required
scalar fields \codeword{report_id}, language and~sha256 use the~same rules as~status. Also
send \codeword{session_id} (1..80 characters [A-Za-z0-9\_-]) and~one binary file in~report.
The \codeword{session_id} is~validated but~is~not~part of~the~stored report identity.
The~actual PDF must match the~submitted SHA-256; valid files are~16 bytes through 25 MiB
(26214400 bytes), with PDF signature, EOF marker and~accepted detected PDF MIME. Hosting
limits may~be~lower.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
PDF_SHA=$(python -c \
 'import hashlib; print(hashlib.sha256(open("report.pdf","rb").read()).hexdigest())')
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_API_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --form 'action=flight_report' --form 'report_id=flight-demo-001' \
  --form 'session_id=session-demo-001' --form 'language=cs' \
  --form "sha256=$PDF_SHA" --form 'report=@report.pdf;type=application/pdf' \
  --write-out '\nHTTP %{http_code}\n' "$API_URL"
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"action":"flight_report","found":true,"conflict":false,
 "report":{"id":"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
 "sha256":"aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa",
 "size":2048,"filename":"flight-report-0123456789abcdef-cs.pdf"}}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 returns a~stored or~already-existing matching receipt. Resubmitting the~same
\codeword{report_id}/language and~identical PDF can~return that receipt; another checksum
for~the~same identity returns 409 and~is~not~overwritten. Missing/incomplete PDF,
validation/storage failure or~checksum mismatch returns 422. After a~lost response, query
\codeword{flight_report_status} with the~original identity/checksum before any deliberate
retry. This specific receipt behavior must not~be~generalized to~ordinary photo upload,
inventory or~arbitrary PDF editing. The~API does not~expose report deletion, replacement
or~media browsing.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Migration: authority and~data contract}{Migration: authority and data contract}}

\label{api352:migration-contract}

Migration is~an~explicit transfer of~the~gallery authorized by~a~source key into a~new child
below the~gallery authorized by~a~destination key. It is~not~server discovery or~a~general
browsing API. Each installation must enable \codeword{gallery_migration} and~have
the~required schema. The~audited \codeword{protocol_version} is~2; source and~destination
\codeword{app_version} strings must match exactly. Preserve the~server-produced manifest,
its identities, order and~optional fields instead of~synthesizing a~manifest from a~local
directory.

The~source export includes descendants by~default. Set \codeword{include_subgalleries}=0
to~request only the~root. This parameter narrows that export request, not~the~permanent
authority of~the~key: a~source asset request may~authorize a~descendant in~the~same source
tree. Treat a~key as~access to~export data, not~merely upload permission. A~target key
authorizes creation of~an~imported child/tree, not~arbitrary root creation or~mutation
of~unrelated galleries. Imported metadata can~retain source visibility; use a~protected
destination and~review the~result before exposing it.

All migration URLs use index.php?page=<route>. GET fields belong in~the~query; POST scalar
fields use application/x-www-form-urlencoded or~multipart/form-data. JSON serialized inside
\codeword{manifest_json} or \codeword{assets_json} is~a~form value, not~an~application/json
request body. On GET/POST routes the~POST field wins when both locations supply the~same
field. Always use a~header credential for~GET. Successful JSON uses application/json;
charset=utf-8. Error payloads have ok=false and~error:string, with action-specific status
codes below. Wrong methods return 405. Do not~depend on~localized error-message wording.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{scope} & Asset selector, string image (default) or~gallery. \\
\codeword{source_gallery_id} & Source gallery integer. Send the~exact manifest value;
source-side zero/omission falls back to~the~key root. Destination references must identify
the~manifest entry. \\
\codeword{kind} & Required string: image original or~thumbnail; gallery
\codeword{cover_image_path}, \codeword{banner_image_path}, \codeword{logo_image_path} or
\codeword{separator_image_path}. \\
\codeword{source_image_id} & Integer for~an~image asset; historical alias
\codeword{image_id} is~accepted when \codeword{source_image_id} is~absent. No image ID
is~needed for~gallery artwork. \\
\codeword{size} / \codeword{format} & Thumbnail identity only: size integer pixels, format
jpg or~webp. Copy the~descriptor, not~a~guessed size. size here is~not~byte length; byte
length is \codeword{file_size}. \\
\codeword{file_size} / \codeword{checksum_sha256} / \codeword{mime_type} & Descriptor
metadata: byte count, SHA-256 digest and~MIME string. Relay unchanged and~verify downloaded
bytes where a~digest is~supplied. \\
\codeword{filename} / \codeword{relative_path} / \codeword{asset_key} / \codeword{label} &
Descriptor values, not~arbitrary path access. \codeword{asset_key} is~an~opaque 24-hex
identity added to~transfer-plan entries; label is~display text. Never construct filesystem
URLs from paths. \\
\end{longtable}

Migration transfers the~assets enumerated by~this protocol, not~a~complete installation
backup. General PDF-document sidecars and~music repositories/playlists are~not~automatically
covered. Do not~invent downloadable URLs or~promise transfer of~unlisted hidden sidecars.
Keep a~separate filesystem/database backup when preserving the~entire installation.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Export the~manifest}{Export the manifest}}

\label{api352:migration-manifest}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
GET|POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_manifest
\end{lstlisting}

Authenticate with the~source gallery key. Optional \codeword{gallery_id} is~a~positive
integer assertion; a~different root returns 403. \codeword{include_subgalleries} defaults
to~true; the~values 0, false, off and~no~(case-insensitive after trimming) select root only.
Success is~HTTP 200 with ok, \codeword{app_version}, \codeword{protocol_version}
and~manifest. Exceptions in~authentication, schema lookup or~manifest generation are~all
mapped to~401 by~this handler; 401 is~therefore not~exclusively a~bad-key diagnosis.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{protocol_version} / \codeword{app_version} & Integer 2 and~exact source
application-version string. The~destination requires the~same application version. \\
\codeword{migration_id} / \codeword{source_instance_id} & Opaque strings generated
by~the~source. Preserve both; the~target uses source/manifest identity to~derive a~resumable
job. \\
\codeword{source_gallery_id} / \codeword{source_gallery_folder} & Integer authorized root ID
and~its gallery-relative folder string, not~an~authorization token or~direct media URL. \\
\codeword{include_subgalleries} / \codeword{generated_at} / \codeword{counts} & Boolean
export selection, server SQL timestamp string and~object \{galleries:integer,
images:integer, assets:integer\}. \\
\codeword{galleries[]} & Parent-first entries: \codeword{source_id},
\codeword{parent_source_id} (root 0), \codeword{source_folder}, \codeword{folder_name},
\codeword{relative_folder} (root empty), gallery, \codeword{gallery_assets}[], images[] and
\codeword{flight_map} (nullable). \\
\codeword{gallery} / \codeword{gallery_assets} / \codeword{images} / \codeword{flight_map} &
Root mirrors retained for~compatibility alongside galleries[]. Preserve them; do~not~merge
different source snapshots. \\
\end{longtable}

The~nested gallery metadata carries only fields present in~storage, plus tags (string),
\codeword{description_layout_semantics_version}, \codeword{cover_source_id} and~optional
\codeword{cover_selection_source_ids} (integer array, including empty Automatic selection).
Optional \codeword{content_language} and~translations depend on~localization schema. Image
metadata includes \codeword{source_id}, \codeword{source_gallery_id}, tags and~assets, plus
present persisted attributes listed below. Numeric database values can~be~strings,
and~optional values can~be~null: do~not~coerce or~translate these version-bound transfer
records. These are~migration records, not~general PATCH operations. Pass the~entire
server-issued object unchanged.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
gallery:
title, slug, description, sort_order, visibility, voting_enabled,
show_filenames, newest_uploads_first, gallery_date, gallery_date_end,
description_layout, count_badge_visibility, picture_game_enabled,
gps_map_enabled, grid_columns, grid_rows, grid_use_for_subgalleries,
thumbnail_min_size, thumbnail_max_size, background_source,
banner_image_path, logo_image_path, separator_image_path,
cover_image_path, nsfw_enabled

images[]:
relative_path, filename, title, description, width, height, mime_type,
file_size, modified_at, checksum_sha256, sort_order, visibility,
exif_taken_at, exif_camera_make, exif_camera_model, exif_lens_model,
exif_focal_length, exif_aperture, exif_exposure_time, exif_iso,
gps_lat, gps_lng, gps_altitude, gps_extracted_at, nsfw_enabled,
thumbnail_min_size, thumbnail_max_size
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
SOURCE_BASE='https://source.example/photos'
: "${PG_SOURCE_KEY:?Set PG_SOURCE_KEY securely}"
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_SOURCE_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --get --data-urlencode 'include_subgalleries=0' \
  "$SOURCE_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_manifest" \
  --output manifest-response.json --write-out 'HTTP %{http_code}\n'
# Validate HTTP/ok before using the file. Extract only the manifest member.
python -c 'import json; r=json.load(open("manifest-response.json")); assert r["ok"] is True; json.dump(r["manifest"],open("manifest.json","w"))'
\end{lstlisting}

When present, \codeword{flight_map} is~an~object with \codeword{map_source_type}:string,
\codeword{route_text}:string, \codeword{resolved_points_json}:string,
\codeword{unresolved_points_json}:string, \codeword{point_count}:integer and
\codeword{resolved_at}:string. The~two *\_json fields contain serialized JSON strings,
not~nested arrays. Keep them unchanged with the~version-bound manifest; no~separate route
creation API is~implied.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Download one source asset}{Download one source asset}}

\label{api352:migration-asset}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
GET|POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_asset
\end{lstlisting}

Use the~source key and~the~asset selectors defined above. The~server resolves the~selected
original, existing thumbnail or~gallery artwork within the~authorized root/tree. A~source
path supplied by~the~caller cannot choose an~arbitrary file. No manifest \codeword{job_id}
is~required on~this source route. Descendant authorization is~checked for~each request,
independently of~whether the~preceding manifest excluded descendants. Success is~HTTP 200
binary with detected Content-Type, Content-Length and~attachment Content-Disposition.
Exceptions, including authentication or~missing files, map to~404 JSON; wrong method is~405.
Do not~save a~JSON error as~a~photograph.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_SOURCE_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --get --data-urlencode 'scope=image' \
  --data-urlencode 'source_gallery_id=12' \
  --data-urlencode 'source_image_id=34' --data-urlencode 'kind=original' \
  "$SOURCE_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_asset" \
  --output source-asset.bin --write-out 'HTTP %{http_code}\n'
# IDs must come from the actual authorized manifest, not this example.
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Download a~source ZIP package}{Download a source ZIP package}}

\label{api352:migration-package}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_package
\end{lstlisting}

Source key, form fields: \codeword{assets_json} (required JSON-encoded nonempty descriptor
array, at~most 2048 assets) and \codeword{include_subgalleries} (optional, true by~default).
Obtain the~exact asset group from the~destination package plan. The~source resolves each
descriptor, verifies available size/checksum evidence and~emits a~ZIP using stored entries
(ZIP compression method 0). ZipArchive support is~required. Success: HTTP 200
application/zip, Content-Length, attachment filename php-gallery-migration-package.zip.
Exceptions including invalid credentials, descriptor/size/hash problems or~ZIP failure
return 422 JSON. Wrong method returns 405.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
# package-assets.json is one packages[i].assets array from the target plan.
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_SOURCE_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --data-urlencode 'assets_json@package-assets.json' \
  --data-urlencode 'include_subgalleries=0' \
  "$SOURCE_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_package" \
  --output transfer.zip --write-out 'HTTP %{http_code}\n'
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Prepare the~destination job}{Prepare the destination job}}

\label{api352:migration-receive-manifest}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_manifest
\end{lstlisting}

Destination gallery key, required form field \codeword{manifest_json} containing only
the~full manifest object from the~source response. This is~a~mutation: it~prepares a~server
job and~the~imported child gallery/tree below the~key gallery. Reuse the~exact manifest
and~destination to~recover a~compatible existing job; do~not~obtain a~changed snapshot
or~choose a~new target after an~ambiguous response. The~server-generated \codeword{job_id}
is~opaque; persist it~with source/destination identity and~manifest, but~without secrets.
Application versions must match exactly and \codeword{protocol_version} must be~supported.
Exceptions (authentication included) return 422 with ok=false/error; wrong method returns
405.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{ok} / \codeword{app_version} / \codeword{job_id} & HTTP 200: true, destination
version string and~job identity string. \\
\codeword{compatibility} & Object \{ok:boolean, \codeword{source_version}:string,
\codeword{target_version}:string, policy:"exact", message:string\}. Incompatible requests
fail before successful preparation. \\
\codeword{target_gallery_id} / \codeword{imported_root_gallery_id} / \codeword{gallery_ids}
& Authorized parent integer; created/imported root integer; array of~imported gallery
integers. Parent and~imported root are~distinct concepts. \\
\codeword{assets[]} / \codeword{packages[]} & Ordered asset descriptors with
\codeword{asset_key}/label; package objects \{\codeword{package_id}:string,
\codeword{asset_keys}:string[], assets:object[], \codeword{asset_count}:integer,
\codeword{source_bytes}:integer\}. Use the~returned plan, not~independently regrouped ZIPs.
\\
\codeword{counts} / \codeword{status} & Counts object \{galleries:integer, images:integer,
assets:integer, packages:integer, received:integer\}. status is~the~job-status object
described below (nullable in~the~controller envelope). \\
\end{longtable}

Package planning respects destination upload limits and~a~soft batch target. One original
plus its derivatives can~exceed the~soft target while staying within the~hard limit; a~group
above the~hard limit is~rejected. Do not~advertise a~universal 24 MiB upload maximum merely
because that is~a~fallback planning target. The~plan may~contain no~assets for~an~empty
gallery, which still needs completion.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
TARGET_BASE='https://target.example/gallery'
: "${PG_TARGET_KEY:?Set PG_TARGET_KEY securely}"
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_TARGET_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --data-urlencode 'manifest_json@manifest.json' \
  "$TARGET_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_manifest" \
  --output job-response.json --write-out 'HTTP %{http_code}\n'
# Preserve the validated job response before transferring any bytes.
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Upload one asset to~the~destination}{Upload one asset to the destination}}

\label{api352:migration-receive-asset}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_asset
\end{lstlisting}

Destination key and~multipart/form-data. Required: \codeword{job_id}, the~asset selector
fields and~a~binary file in~asset. Selectors must identify a~planned manifest asset;
the~server checks job ownership, descriptor, content size/hash and~destination safety.
Transfer the~returned asset order so~originals precede their thumbnails. Gallery artwork
requires scope=gallery and~its exact kind. Success is~HTTP 200 with ok=true,
\codeword{job_id}, \codeword{asset_key}, \codeword{target_gallery_id} (the~imported gallery
receiving this asset), result, received and \codeword{total_assets}. This
\codeword{target_gallery_id} can~differ from the~key parent. Exceptions including invalid
credentials, unknown job/asset, missing upload or~checksum mismatch return 422; wrong method
405.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Field} & \textbf{Type, requirement and~meaning} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
\codeword{result: image original} & \{scope:"image", kind:"original",
\codeword{source_image_id}:integer, \codeword{image_id}:integer\}; source and~destination
IDs differ. \\
\codeword{result: image thumbnail} & \{scope:"image", kind:"thumbnail",
\codeword{source_image_id}:integer, \codeword{image_id}:integer, size:integer,
format:string\}. \\
\codeword{result: gallery} & \{scope:"gallery", kind:string,
\codeword{relative_path}:string\}; kind is~the~artwork column from the~descriptor. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"job_id":"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
 "asset_key":"0123456789abcdef01234567","target_gallery_id":41,
 "result":{"scope":"image","kind":"original",
 "source_image_id":34,"image_id":100},"received":1,"total_assets":3}
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Upload a~planned ZIP package}{Upload a planned ZIP package}}

\label{api352:migration-receive-package}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_package
\end{lstlisting}

Destination key, multipart/form-data: \codeword{job_id} and \codeword{package_id}
are~required strings from preparation; package is~the~required binary ZIP. No
\codeword{assets_json} is~accepted as~a~replacement destination plan. Send the~unmodified
ZIP generated by~the~source for~that exact package. The~receiver checks expected entry
names/count, rejects duplicate/unexpected entries and~compressed entries (only stored method
0), verifies asset evidence and~uses its authorized job mapping. Some assets
can~be~installed before a~later failure; this is~not~an~atomic rollback protocol. After
an~ambiguous upload, call \codeword{receive_status} for~the~same job before deciding
to~resend.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"job_id":"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
 "package_id":"abcdef0123456789abcdef01",
 "asset_keys":["0123456789abcdef01234567"],
 "received":1,"total_assets":1}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 fields above contain strings/string arrays and~integer counts. Authentication,
job/plan mismatch, invalid ZIP, missing package, wrong content or~incomplete transfer
exceptions return 422 JSON. Wrong method returns 405. A~package already recorded as~received
is~not~permission to~switch its content or~move it~to~a~different job. Use current status
and~original package identity for~recovery.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Reconcile destination progress}{Reconcile destination progress}}

\label{api352:migration-receive-status}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
GET|POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_status
\end{lstlisting}

Destination key and~required \codeword{job_id}. Optionally include the~six asset-selector
fields to~ask about one manifest asset. The~same target-parent ownership is~checked on~every
call. This handler reconciles stored receipt evidence against files; even GET can~update
recovery bookkeeping, so~do~not~describe it~as~a~side-effect-free snapshot.
Missing/unmatched optional asset selection produces \codeword{asset_key}="" and
\codeword{asset_received}=false rather than a~global file-absence guarantee. Invalid
key/job/target, corrupt job or~reconciliation exceptions return 422; wrong method 405.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"action":"status",
 "job_id":"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef","mode":"source_push",
 "target_gallery_id":40,"imported_root_gallery_id":41,"gallery_ids":[41],
 "received":1,"total_assets":3,"received_packages":0,"total_packages":1,
 "received_asset_keys":["0123456789abcdef01234567"],
 "asset_key":"","asset_received":false,
 "updated_at":"2026-11-09 12:00:00","server_time":"2026-11-09 12:00:03"}
\end{lstlisting}

The~complete HTTP 200 shape is~shown above. IDs/counts are~integers, \codeword{gallery_ids}
is~an~integer array, \codeword{received_asset_keys} is~a~string array, times are~server SQL
strings, and \codeword{asset_received} is~boolean. \codeword{received_packages} counts
packages for~which every constituent asset is~received. mode is \codeword{source_push}
for~jobs started through this public preparation route. There is~no~completed flag in~this
response. received==\codeword{total_assets} means bytes are~accounted for, not~that final
metadata application succeeded; call \codeword{receive_complete} separately.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Complete the~destination job}{Complete the destination job}}

\label{api352:migration-receive-complete}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
POST index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_complete
\end{lstlisting}

Destination key and~required form field \codeword{job_id}, no~file. The~server requires
the~authorized target job, all expected assets and~usable schema, then applies metadata,
source-to-target image/cover mappings and~related finalization. This can~change the~imported
gallery presentation/visibility. A~lost success response is~not~proof of~rollback. Preserve
the~original job and~inspect its result; do~not~start another import merely because
completion was~ambiguous. No public cancel/delete-job or~rollback endpoint is~defined
by~this contract.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":true,"job_id":"0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
 "target_gallery_id":40,"target_parent_gallery_id":40,
 "imported_root_gallery_id":41,"gallery_ids":[41],
 "assets_received":3,"total_assets":3,
 "gallery_url":"https://target.example/gallery/gallery/imported/",
 "edit_url":"https://target.example/gallery/index.php?page=admin_edit_gallery&id=41&tab=admin-edit-api#admin-edit-api"}
\end{lstlisting}

HTTP 200 contains the~fields above; IDs/counts are~integers, \codeword{gallery_ids}
is~an~integer array and~URLs are~strings (use the~returned values, not~this illustrative
spelling). Authentication, incomplete assets, target/schema/metadata failures return 422
with ok=false/error, wrong method 405. The~Admin \codeword{edit_url} is~a~browser link
requiring its normal Admin authentication; it~does not~grant API-key access to~Admin
functions.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{HTTP errors, limits and~compatibility}{HTTP errors, limits and compatibility}}

\label{api352:errors}

The~following matrix describes handler behavior on~the~reviewed baseline, after the~request
reaches PHP. It is~not~a~uniform REST status policy. Disabled-feature routing, invalid
installation/schema state, PHP body limits, a~reverse proxy or~an~upstream error can~produce
another status or~HTML before the~handler. Send Accept: application/json; validate status,
Content-Type and~JSON shape independently. Do not~infer write rollback from any HTTP error
or~failed JSON parse. Do not~branch on~translated free-form error messages.

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.28\linewidth}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{0.67\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Operation group} & \textbf{Failure interpretation} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\textbf{Operation group} & \textbf{Failure interpretation} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
All upload actions & 405 wrong method; 503 token schema unavailable/unverifiable; 401
missing/invalid/revoked key; 404 key gallery missing. These occur before action dispatch. \\
\codeword{upload} / \codeword{inventory} / \codeword{revoke} & Upload: 403 positive gallery
assertion mismatch, 422 caught upload failure. Revoke: 422 failed revocation. Inventory
has~no~dedicated local exception-to-status mapping; infrastructure exceptions need
not~produce the~usual JSON envelope. \\
\codeword{ai_*} & 403 disabled AI feature, 409 missing queue schema, 503 unverifiable
schema, 400 unknown AI action, 422 caught AI failure; heartbeat/complete/asset 409
for~an~invalid claim or~unavailable claimed input. \\
\codeword{flight_report_status} / \codeword{flight_report} & Status: 400 missing JSON body;
both: 409 conflicting PDF identity, 422 invalid input or~status/storage failure. \\
\codeword{gallery_migration_manifest} & 403 root assertion mismatch; 401 any caught
exception including invalid key, unavailable schema or~export failure; 405 wrong method. \\
\codeword{gallery_migration_asset} & 404 caught authentication/resolution/read preparation
failure; 405 wrong method. A~failure after streaming begins need not~be~recoverable as~clean
JSON. \\
\codeword{gallery_migration_package} / \codeword{gallery_migration_receive_*} & 422 any
caught exception including key/target/job/version/payload/ZIP/integrity problems; 405 wrong
method. Authentication errors are~not~universally 401 on~these routes. \\
\end{longtable}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":false,"error":"Invalid or revoked API key."}
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
{"ok":false,"action":"flight_report_status","conflict":true,
 "error":"A different PDF is already stored for this report identity."}
\end{lstlisting}

These are~separate error examples, not~a~universal shape requiring action or~conflict.
For~a~key-only caller without Admin cookies, a~disabled \codeword{upload_api} or
\codeword{gallery_migration} route normally returns 404 with
\{"ok":false,"error":"\codeword{not_found}"\} when JSON is~requested; an~authenticated Admin
context can~instead get 403 \codeword{feature_disabled}. Keep API calls independent
of~browser sessions. The~two JSON helpers are~not~identical: upload uses application/json,
migration explicitly adds charset=utf-8. Do not~assume all success/error replies set
identical cache/security headers. Treat all authenticated metadata as~private and~never
cache credentials.

Operational limits are~the~intersection of~application validation, installed feature/schema
support, PHP
\codeword{upload_max_filesize}/\codeword{post_max_size}/\codeword{max_file_uploads},
web-server body/time limits and~client memory/time limits. A~host can~return 413 or~429
without those being uniform application-level guarantees. No universal Retry-After,
pagination contract, resumable multipart upload, chunk API, rate quota or~exactly-once
guarantee is~defined here. Retain all original files and~uncertain-operation evidence.
Inventory and~report receipts have different meanings. Respect optional/missing response
fields on~older servers; no~general API-version endpoint is~promised. Migration alone
requires exact application-version equality.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Complete Python upload, inventory and~migration example}{Complete Python upload, inventory and migration example}}

\label{api352:complete-client}

Save the~following complete script as \codeword{gallery_api_example}.py. It needs Python
3.10+ and~no~third-party package. \codeword{PG_BASE}, \codeword{PG_SOURCE_BASE} and
\codeword{PG_TARGET_BASE} are~explicit installation-base URLs including any subdirectory,
without index.php, query or~fragment. Supply keys in~environment variables only. The~example
deliberately bounds each file/binary response to~64 MiB and~JSON to~8 MiB, with a~120-second
socket timeout; these are~client-example limits, not~server limits. It buffers bounded files
and~uses ASCII multipart filenames; larger or~differently named uploads require
an~appropriately streaming/encoding-aware client. urllib may~use the~configured operator
proxy. TLS verification stays enabled and~every redirect is~refused.

The~migrate command performs a~complete new manifest -> destination preparation -> ordered
per-asset transfer -> completion sequence for~the~explicitly selected source/target keys. It
uses individual-asset routes so~a~ZIP tool is~unnecessary; the~package routes above provide
a~separate batching option. It refuses an~existing work directory rather than accidentally
starting a~second import. Private manifest/job files survive an~error; they may~contain
private gallery metadata, but~never credentials. After an~error, inspect the~saved job
and~query its status manually. Do not~simply rerun into a~different directory. Missing
job-response.json after preparation failure still requires reconciling the~original saved
manifest and~target before another attempt. The~script never removes source photographs
or~automatically retries writes. Printed upload JSON can~contain warnings even when ok=true.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
"""Example client for the documented gallery-key API. Author: Rudolf Klusal.

Requires Python 3.10+ and the standard library. It never follows redirects,
retries writes, deletes original files, or enumerates unrelated galleries.
The bounds below belong to this example, not to PHP Gallery's API contract.
"""

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import secrets
import ssl
import sys
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request

# Example-only memory/time bounds; stop explicitly when an input is larger.
FILE_LIMIT = 64 * 1024 * 1024
JSON_LIMIT = 8 * 1024 * 1024
TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 120
UPLOAD_ROUTE = "upload_automation_upload"
ASSET_FIELDS = (
    "scope", "source_gallery_id", "kind", "source_image_id", "size", "format"
)


class NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    """Refuse all redirect responses so credentials never follow a new URL."""

    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
        """Return no replacement request; urllib reports an HTTP error."""
        return None


class Api:
    """Call fixed gallery routes with one installation-specific key."""

    def __init__(self, base: str, key: str) -> None:
        """Validate the explicit HTTPS installation base and retain its key."""
        parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(base)
        if (parsed.scheme != "https" or not parsed.hostname
                or parsed.username or parsed.password
                or parsed.query or parsed.fragment or not key):
            raise ValueError("Use an HTTPS installation base and a nonempty key")
        self.base = base.rstrip("/")
        self.key = key
        self.opener = urllib.request.build_opener(
            NoRedirect(), urllib.request.HTTPSHandler(
                context=ssl.create_default_context()
            )
        )

    def call(self, route: str, fields: dict | None = None,
             payload: dict | None = None,
             upload: tuple[str, str, bytes] | None = None,
             binary: bool = False) -> dict | bytes:
        """POST once; return verified JSON success or binary bytes, else raise.

        :param route: Fixed documented front-controller route.
        :param fields: Scalar form fields, not JSON request-body fields.
        :param payload: JSON body; mutually exclusive with fields/upload.
        :param upload: Multipart (field name, ASCII filename, file bytes).
        :param binary: Read a bounded binary attachment instead of JSON.
        :return: Successful JSON object or received binary bytes.
        :raises RuntimeError: HTTP, transport, body-limit or JSON failure.
        """
        if payload is not None and (fields is not None or upload is not None):
            raise ValueError("JSON and form/multipart cannot be combined")
        headers = {"X-Gallery-API-Key": self.key, "Accept": "application/json"}
        if upload is not None:
            field, filename, content = upload
            if len(content) > FILE_LIMIT:
                raise ValueError("File exceeds the example's memory bound")
            filename.encode("ascii")
            if any(c in field + filename for c in '\r\n"\\'):
                raise ValueError("Unsafe multipart field/filename")
            boundary = "pg-example-" + secrets.token_hex(16)
            chunks = []
            for name, value in (fields or {}).items():
                chunks.append((f"--{boundary}\r\nContent-Disposition: "
                               f'form-data; name="{name}"\r\n\r\n'
                               f"{value}\r\n").encode("utf-8"))
            chunks.extend([
                (f"--{boundary}\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; "
                 f'name="{field}"; filename="{filename}"\r\n'
                 "Content-Type: application/octet-stream\r\n\r\n").encode(),
                content, f"\r\n--{boundary}--\r\n".encode(),
            ])
            data = b"".join(chunks)
            headers["Content-Type"] = f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"
        elif payload is not None:
            data = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
            headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"
        else:
            data = urllib.parse.urlencode(fields or {}).encode("ascii")
            headers["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
        url = self.base + "/index.php?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({"page": route})
        request = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, headers=headers,
                                         method="POST")
        try:
            with self.opener.open(request, timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS) as response:
                limit = FILE_LIMIT if binary else JSON_LIMIT
                body = response.read(limit + 1)
                mime = response.headers.get_content_type()
                if response.status != 200 or len(body) > limit:
                    raise RuntimeError("Unexpected status or oversized response")
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
            status = exc.code
            exc.close()
            raise RuntimeError(f"HTTP {status}; no automatic retry") from None
        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:
            raise RuntimeError("Transport failed; write outcome may be unknown") from exc
        if binary:
            if mime in ("application/json", "text/html"):
                raise RuntimeError("Expected attachment, received JSON/HTML")
            return body
        if mime != "application/json":
            raise RuntimeError("Expected JSON, received another media type")
        result = json.loads(body)
        if not isinstance(result, dict) or result.get("ok") is not True:
            raise RuntimeError("Server did not confirm ok=true; inspect safely")
        return result


def read_file(path: Path) -> bytes:
    """Read one bounded original without modifying it; raise if too large."""
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        content = stream.read(FILE_LIMIT + 1)
    if len(content) > FILE_LIMIT:
        raise ValueError("File exceeds the example's 64 MiB limit")
    return content


def save_json(path: Path, value: dict) -> None:
    """Exclusively save non-secret transfer metadata in the private workdir."""
    with path.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        json.dump(value, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        stream.write("\n")


def migrate(directory: Path, include_children: bool) -> dict:
    """Run a new explicit migration; preserve evidence and stop on any failure.

    :param directory: New private directory; an existing directory is refused.
    :param include_children: Explicitly include the authorized source tree.
    :return: Successful completion receipt. Never retries ambiguous writes.
    """
    source = Api(os.environ["PG_SOURCE_BASE"], os.environ["PG_SOURCE_KEY"])
    target = Api(os.environ["PG_TARGET_BASE"], os.environ["PG_TARGET_KEY"])
    directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=False, exist_ok=False)
    envelope = source.call("gallery_migration_manifest", {
        "include_subgalleries": "1" if include_children else "0"
    })
    save_json(directory / "source-response.json", envelope)
    manifest = envelope["manifest"]
    # This source snapshot is retained BEFORE any destination mutation.
    save_json(directory / "manifest.json", manifest)
    prepared = target.call("gallery_migration_receive_manifest", {
        "manifest_json": json.dumps(manifest)
    })
    save_json(directory / "job-response.json", prepared)
    job_id = prepared["job_id"]
    status = target.call("gallery_migration_receive_status", {"job_id": job_id})
    received = set(status["received_asset_keys"])
    for asset in prepared["assets"]:
        if asset["asset_key"] in received:
            continue
        selectors = {k: asset[k] for k in ASSET_FIELDS if k in asset}
        content = source.call("gallery_migration_asset", selectors, binary=True)
        expected_size = int(asset.get("file_size") or 0)
        expected_hash = asset.get("checksum_sha256") or ""
        if expected_size > 0 and len(content) != expected_size:
            raise RuntimeError("Source size changed; stop and retain the job")
        if expected_hash and hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() != expected_hash:
            raise RuntimeError("Source checksum changed; stop and retain the job")
        target.call("gallery_migration_receive_asset",
                    {"job_id": job_id, **selectors},
                    upload=("asset", "asset.bin", content))
    result = target.call("gallery_migration_receive_complete", {"job_id": job_id})
    save_json(directory / "completion.json", result)
    return result


def main() -> None:
    """Dispatch one explicit upload/inventory or a new authorized migration."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("operation", choices=("upload", "inventory", "migrate"))
    parser.add_argument("path", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--include-children", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.operation == "migrate":
        result = migrate(args.path, args.include_children)
    else:
        client = Api(os.environ["PG_BASE"], os.environ["PG_API_KEY"])
        content = read_file(args.path)
        filename = args.path.name
        if args.operation == "upload":
            result = client.call(UPLOAD_ROUTE, {"action": "upload"},
                                 upload=("image", filename, content))
        else:
            result = client.call(UPLOAD_ROUTE, payload={
                "action": "inventory", "files": [{
                    "client_id": "example-1", "filename": filename,
                    "size": len(content),
                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(),
                }]
            })
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except (KeyError, ValueError, OSError, RuntimeError) as error:
        detail = str(error) if isinstance(error, RuntimeError) else type(error).__name__
        print(f"Stopped: {detail}. Preserve inputs and inspect "
              "the original gallery/job; do not blindly repeat writes.",
              file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)
\end{lstlisting}

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
export PG_BASE='https://gallery.example/photos'
: "${PG_API_KEY:?Set securely}"
export PG_API_KEY
python gallery_api_example.py inventory photo.jpg
# The next command stores a photograph. Do not blindly repeat it.
python gallery_api_example.py upload photo.jpg

export PG_SOURCE_BASE='https://source.example/photos'
export PG_TARGET_BASE='https://target.example/gallery'
: "${PG_SOURCE_KEY:?Set securely}" "${PG_TARGET_KEY:?Set securely}"
# Keys must be exported into the child process environment.
export PG_API_KEY PG_SOURCE_KEY PG_TARGET_KEY
# Creates a child in the target; the workdir must not exist.
python gallery_api_example.py migrate ./migration-evidence
# Add --include-children only for an explicitly authorized subtree transfer.
\end{lstlisting}

For~a~retained job, the~following status request does not~create a~new import.
\codeword{JOB_ID} is~copied from the~validated job-response.json. Decide how to~recover from
the~returned evidence; absence of~a~completion flag is~intentional. A~finalization request
is~a~separate deliberate mutation, not~a~harmless connectivity test.

\begin{lstlisting}[language={}]
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_TARGET_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' --data-urlencode "job_id=$JOB_ID" \
  "$TARGET_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_status"
# Only after reviewing the original job and its complete asset receipts:
curl --silent --show-error --proto '=https' --max-time 120 \
  --header "X-Gallery-API-Key: $PG_TARGET_KEY" \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' --data-urlencode "job_id=$JOB_ID" \
  "$TARGET_BASE/index.php?page=gallery_migration_receive_complete"
\end{lstlisting}

Preserve comments/docstrings and~review Markdown plus all four manual editions when changing
these wire contracts. A~branch-only implementation is~not~automatically a~supported released
API. Do not~add automatic browsing, server-wide listing or~browser-approved APIs merely
because their routes exist. docs/API.MD is~the~English reference; the~corresponding manual
chapters contain the~same technical content in~the~edition language.
